Samsonite International S.A.
新秀麗國際有限公司
Stock Code 股份代號 1910




INTERIM REPORT 中期報告 2024
                              We are encouraged by Samsonite’s resilient results in the first
                           half of 2024. The Group recorded consolidated net sales of
                        US$1,768.5 million, a 2.8% constant currency increase versus the
                     corresponding period in 2023, underscoring our resilient business
                   model, enhanced margin profile, and ongoing discipline on expense
                 management.


          我們對新秀麗於 2024 年上半年的強韌業績感到鼓舞。本集團錄得綜合
        銷售淨額 1,768.5 百萬美元，按不變匯率基準計算較 2023 年同期增長
     2.8%，突顯了我們富有彈性的業務模式、利潤率的提升及對開支的持
    續嚴格管理。




Timothy Charles Parker
Chairman 主席

CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
With a heritage dating back to 1910, Samsonite International S.A. (the      新秀麗國際有限公司（「本公司」，連同其綜合附屬
“Company”), together with its consolidated subsidiaries (the “Group”),      公司統稱為「本集團」）的悠久歷史可追溯至 1910
is a leader in the global lifestyle bag industry and is the world’s best-   年，是全球時尚箱包行業的翹楚，並且是全球最著
known and largest travel luggage company. The Group is principally          名、規模最大的行李箱公司。本集團主要在全球
engaged in the design, manufacture, sourcing and distribution of            從事設計、製造、採購及分銷行李箱、商務包及電
luggage, business and computer bags, outdoor and casual bags                腦包、戶外包及休閒包以及旅遊配件，旗下品牌主
and travel accessories throughout the world, primarily under the            要包括新秀麗 ®、Tumi ®、American Tourister ®、
Samsonite ®, Tumi ®, American Tourister ®, Gregory ®, High Sierra ®,        Gregory ®、High Sierra ®、Lipault ® 及Hartmann ®
Lipault ® and Hartmann ® brand names as well as other owned and             品牌以及其他自有及獲授權的品牌。
licensed brand names.


THE COMPANY’S CULTURE                                                       本公司的文化及策略
AND STRATEGY
The principal characteristics of the Company’s culture are:                 本公司文化的主要特點是：

•      The Company’s long-standing guiding principle, the Golden            •       本公司一直以來的指導原則以「己所不欲，
       Rule, to “do unto others as you would have them do unto you,”                勿施於人」為黃金法則，這是本集團創始人
       is a legacy of the Group’s founder and guides relationships                  的遺訓，指導着本集團員工與其客戶、供應
       between the Group’s employees and its customers, vendors,                    商和其他持份者之間的關係。
       and other stakeholders.

•      The Group’s decentralized management model empowers                  •       本集團的分權管理模式為團隊賦能，同時鼓
       teams and fosters an entrepreneurial, innovative approach to                 勵合作共享最佳實踐，促進以創業且創新的
       the business while encouraging collaboration to share best                   精神經營業務。
       practices.

•      The Group’s lean organizational structure encourages efficient,      •       本集團的精簡組織架構鼓勵高效、及時的決
       timely decision-making and transparency, and minimizes                       策和透明度，並盡量減少企業的官僚主義。
       corporate bureaucracy.

•      The Group’s employees have a shared commitment to                    •       本集團的員工共同承諾，將按照本公司的行
       acting lawfully, ethically and responsibly in keeping with                   為守則合法、合道德和負責任地行事，該守
       the Company’s code of conduct, which is communicated to                      則每年都會向整個集團的員工傳達。
       employees throughout the Group annually.

•      The Group is focused on fostering a culture that embraces            •       本集團致力於培養崇尚多元共融的文化。
       diversity and inclusion.

•      Employees’ shared commitment to the Company’s “Our                   •       員工共同承諾推行本公司的「負責任之旅」
       Responsible Journey” sustainability initiatives.                             可持續發展倡議。

THE COMPANY’S CULTURE AND STRATEGY
本公司的文化及策略

The Company believes that its culture is critical to successfully          本公司認為公司文化對成功執行策略至關重要，並
executing its strategies and is well aligned with such strategies.         與有關策略配合接軌。


The Company aims to increase shareholder value through sustainable         本公司旨在透過可持續收益及盈利增長以及產生自
revenue and earnings growth and free cash flow generation. To              由現金流量以提升股東價值。為達到此目標，本公
achieve this objective, the Company has adopted the following principal    司已採納以下主要策略：
strategies:

•      Ensure the Company’s well-diversified family of brands attracts     •   確保本公司的多元化品牌組合吸引旅遊及非
       consumers at a wide range of price points in the travel and             旅遊行李箱、箱包及配件類別廣泛的價格點
       non-travel luggage, bag and accessories categories.                     的消費者。


•      Increase the proportion of net sales from the Company’s direct-     •   提升本公司直接面向消費者電子商貿渠道在
       to-consumer e-commerce channel.                                         銷售淨額中的佔比。


•      Increase investment in marketing to support the Company’s           •   增加營銷投資，以支持本公司的品牌及發展
       brands and initiatives.                                                 方案。


•      Continue to leverage the Company’s regional management              •   繼續憑藉本公司的地區管理架構、分銷專長
       structure, distribution expertise and marketing engine to extend        以及營銷動力，將其品牌拓展至新市場，並
       its brands into new markets and penetrate deeper into existing          加深滲透現有渠道。
       channels.

•      Continue to invest in research and development to develop           •   繼續投資於研究與開發，以開發更輕巧及更
       lighter and stronger new materials, advanced manufacturing              堅固的新物料、先進的製造技術、具吸引力
       processes, exciting new designs, more sustainable collections           的新設計、更多可持續產品系列以及為消費
       and innovative functionalities that deliver tangible benefits to        者帶來切實效益的創新功能。
       consumers.

•      Continue to incorporate the Company’s environmental, social         •    透過落實本公司「負責任之旅」，繼續在本
       and governance (“ESG”) philosophy into its core business                 公司的核心業務中貫徹其環境、社會及管治
       practices through “Our Responsible Journey” to lead the                 （「環境、社會及管治」）理念，引領行李箱
       transformation of the luggage industry with respect to                   行業可持續發展轉型，並繼續公平對待及尊
       sustainability and treat all stakeholders with fairness and              重所有持份者。
       respect.

•      While growth in global travel and tourism is expected to remain     •   儘管全球旅遊業及觀光業預計於2024 年維
       healthy throughout 2024, the Group is seeing a more uncertain           持穩健增長，本集團面臨的宏觀經濟環境越
       macroeconomic environment with softening consumer                       來越不明朗，北美洲及亞洲主要市場的消費

       continues to see softer sales trends heading into the second            集團繼續看到銷售趨勢放緩。面對嚴峻的宏
       half of the year. In this tougher macroeconomic environment,            觀經濟環境，本集團的首要任務仍然是推動
       the Group’s priority remains to drive high-quality sales to build       高質量銷售，為持久盈利且品牌不斷增值的
       a strong foundation for long-term, profitable, brand-accretive          發展奠定穩固基礎。
       growth.

•      The Group is targeting advertising spend at approximately 7% of     •   本集團以廣告支出佔銷售淨額約7%為目
       its net sales to drive future net sales growth across all brands.       標，以推動所有品牌未來銷售淨額的增長。


•      The Group expects to maintain its higher profitability by           •   本集團預計藉着努力管理開支，加上透過其
       diligently managing expenses and driving profitable sales               利潤率較高的品牌、渠道及地區推動可盈利
       growth through its higher-margin brands, channels and                   銷售的增長，從而維持更高的盈利能力。
       regions.

Notes 註釋

(1) Free Cash Flow is defined as net cash generated from (used in) operating activities less (i) purchases of property, plant and equipment
    and software (“total capital expenditures”) and (ii) principal payments on lease liabilities (each as set forth on the condensed consolidated
    statements of cash flows).
    自由現金流量定義為經營活動所得（所用）現金淨額減去(i)購置物業、廠房及設備以及採購軟件費用（「資本開支總額」）及(ii)租賃負債的本金付款（分
    別如簡明綜合現金流量表所載）。

(2) Total liquidity is calculated as the sum of cash and cash equivalents per the condensed consolidated statements of financial position plus
    available capacity under the revolving credit facility (see Management Discussion and Analysis – Indebtedness for further discussion). As of
    June 30, 2024, the Group had total liquidity of US$1,559.6 million, comprising cash and cash equivalents of US$815.5 million and US$744.1
    million available to be borrowed on the Group’s revolving credit facility.
    流動資金總額是按簡明綜合財務狀況表內的現金及現金等價物，加上就循環信貸融通（進一步討論請參閱管理層討論與分析 － 負債）項下可獲得的融
    資額兩者總和計算。於2024年6月30日，本集團的流動資金總額為1,559.6百萬美元，當中包括現金及現金等價物815.5百萬美元及本集團循環信貸融
    通項下可供借入的744.1百萬美元。

Notes 註釋
(1) Results stated on a constant currency basis, a non-IFRS measure, are calculated by applying the average exchange rate of the same period in
    the year under comparison to current period local currency results.
    按不變匯率基準計算呈列的業績為非IFRS計量工具，是以當地貨幣呈列的本期間業績採用與其相比較年度同期的平均匯率計算所得。

(2) Adjusted Net Income, a non-IFRS measure, eliminates the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash
    charges, along with their respective tax effects, that impact the Group’s reported profit attributable to the equity holders, which the Group
    believes helps to give securities analysts, investors and other interested parties a more complete understanding of the Group’s underlying
    financial performance. See Management Discussion and Analysis – Adjusted Net Income for a reconciliation from the Group’s profit
    attributable to the equity holders to Adjusted Net Income.
    經調整淨收入為非IFRS計量工具，其撇除影響本集團呈報的股權持有人應佔溢利的多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用（連同其各自的稅
    務影響）的影響，本集團相信其有助證券分析員、投資者及其他利益相關方更全面了解本集團的相關財務表現。有關本集團股權持有人應佔溢利與經
    調整淨收入的對賬，請參閱管理層討論與分析 － 經調整淨收入。

(3) Adjusted EBITDA, a non-IFRS measure, eliminates the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash charges.
    Adjusted EBITDA includes the lease interest and amortization expense under IFRS 16, Leases (“IFRS 16”) to account for operational rent
    expenses. The Group believes these measures provide additional information that is useful in gaining a more complete understanding of its
    operational performance and of the underlying trends of its business. See Management Discussion and Analysis – Adjusted EBITDA for a
    reconciliation from the Group’s profit for the period to Adjusted EBITDA.
    經調整EBITDA為非IFRS計量工具，其撇除多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用的影響。經調整EBITDA包括IFRS第16號租賃（「IFRS第16
    號」）項下租賃利息及攤銷開支，以便計入經營租金開支。本集團相信該等計量工具會提供更多資訊，有利於更全面了解其經營表現及其業務的相關趨
    勢。有關本集團期內溢利與經調整EBITDA的對賬，請參閱管理層討論與分析 － 經調整EBITDA。

8        Samsonite International S.A. Interim Report 2024
         新秀麗國際有限公司 2024年中期報告
(4) Adjusted EBITDA margin, a non-IFRS measure, is calculated by dividing Adjusted EBITDA by net sales.
    經調整EBITDA利潤率為非IFRS計量工具，以經調整EBITDA除以銷售淨額計算所得。

(5) Adjusted basic and diluted earnings per share, both non-IFRS measures, are calculated by dividing Adjusted Net Income by the weighted
    average number of shares used in the basic and diluted earnings per share calculations, respectively.
    經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利均為非IFRS計量工具，是以經調整淨收入分別除以每股基本及攤薄盈利計算所用的加權平均股份數目計算所得。

    and software (“total capital expenditures”) and (ii) principal payments on lease liabilities (each as set forth on the condensed consolidated
    statements of cash flows).
    自由現金流量定義為經營活動所得（所用）現金淨額減去(i)購置物業、廠房及設備以及採購軟件費用（「資本開支總額」）及(ii)租賃負債的本金付款（分
    別如簡明綜合現金流量表所載）。

(2) Total liquidity is calculated as the sum of cash and cash equivalents per the condensed consolidated statements of financial position plus
    available capacity under the revolving credit facility.
    流動資金總額是按簡明綜合財務狀況表內的現金及現金等價物，加上就循環信貸融通項下可獲得的融資額兩者總和計算。

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
管理層討論與分析
With a heritage dating back to 1910, Samsonite International S.A. (the      新秀麗國際有限公司（「本公司」，連同其綜合附屬
“Company”), together with its consolidated subsidiaries (the “Group”),      公司統稱為「本集團」）的悠久歷史可追溯至 1910
is a leader in the global lifestyle bag industry and is the world’s best-   年，是全球時尚箱包行業的翹楚，並且是全球最著
known and largest travel luggage company. The Group is principally          名、規模最大的行李箱公司。本集團主要在全球
engaged in the design, manufacture, sourcing and distribution of            從事設計、製造、採購及分銷行李箱、商務包及電
luggage, business and computer bags, outdoor and casual bags                腦包、戶外包及休閒包以及旅遊配件，旗下品牌主
and travel accessories throughout the world, primarily under the            要包括新秀麗 ®、Tumi ®、American Tourister ®、
Samsonite ®, Tumi ®, American Tourister ®, Gregory ®, High Sierra ®,        Gregory ®、High Sierra ®、Lipault ® 及Hartmann ®
Lipault ® and Hartmann ® brand names as well as other owned and             品牌以及其他自有及獲授權的品牌。
licensed brand names.

The Group sells its products in over 100 countries through a variety of     本集團透過各種批發分銷渠道、自營零售店及電子
wholesale distribution channels, through its company-operated retail        商貿在超過100個國家銷售產品。
stores and through e-commerce.

Management discussion and analysis should be read in conjunction             管理層討論與分析應與根據國際會計準則理事會
with the Group’s consolidated interim financial statements, which have      （「IASB」）頒佈的《國際財務報告準則》
                                                                                                （「IFRS」）會
been prepared in accordance with International Financial Reporting           計準則編製的本集團綜合中期財務報表一併閱讀。
Standards (“IFRS”) Accounting Standards as issued by the International
Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”).

The Company has presented certain non-IFRS measures within                  本公司於管理層討論與分析呈列若干非IFRS計量工
Management Discussion and Analysis because each of these measures           具，因上述各計量工具提供更多資訊，管理層相信
provides additional information that management believes is useful          有利於證券分析員、投資者及其他利益相關方更全
for securities analysts, investors and other interested parties to gain a   面了解本集團的經營表現及影響其業務的趨勢。本
more complete understanding of the Group’s operational performance          文所計算的此等非IFRS計量工具未必可與其他公司
and of the trends impacting its business. These non-IFRS measures,          所使用類似命名的計量工具進行比較，且不應被視
as calculated herein, may not be comparable to similarly named              為可與IFRS計量工具比較的計量工具。非IFRS計量
measures used by other companies and should not be considered               工具作為一項分析工具有其局限性，不應被視為獨
comparable to IFRS measures. Non-IFRS measures have limitations             立於或代替本集團根據IFRS會計準則所呈報的財務
as an analytical tool and should not be considered in isolation from,       業績的分析。
or as a substitute for, an analysis of the Group’s financial results as
reported under IFRS Accounting Standards.

    香港呈報的銷售淨額包括於澳門錄得的銷售淨額。

(1) The geographic location of the Group’s net sales generally reflects the country from which its products were sold and does not necessarily

    不包括對來自境外對巴西的分銷商作出的任何銷售。
(3) The net sales figure for the “Other” geographic location includes sales in Argentina, Colombia, Panama, Peru, Uruguay and sales to third-
    party distributors in Brazil from outside of Brazil.
   「其他」地域位置的銷售淨額數據包括於阿根廷、哥倫比亞、巴拿馬、秘魯及烏拉圭作出的銷售以及自巴西境外向巴西境內的第三方分銷商作出的銷售。
(4) When excluding the net sales in Argentina, which experienced higher inflation and currency depreciation, the Group’s net sales in Latin America
    for the six months ended June 30, 2024, increased by 5.7% on a constant currency basis, compared to the six months ended June 30, 2023.
    經撇除於阿根廷的銷售淨額（當地經歷較高通貨膨脹及貨幣貶值），本集團截至2024年6月30日止六個月於拉丁美洲的銷售淨額較截至2023年6月30日
    止六個月增加5.7%（按不變匯率基準計算）。

liens, (iv) sell assets and subsidiary stock, (v) pay dividends or make      息或作出其他分派，或者回購或贖回本公司或其受
other distributions or repurchase or redeem the capital stock or             限制附屬公司的股本或次級債務；(vi)與聯屬公司進
subordinated debt of the Company or its restricted subsidiaries,             行若干交易；(vii)訂立限制附屬公司派付股息或限
(vi) engage in certain transactions with affiliates, (vii) enter into        制償付公司間貸款和放款的協議；(viii)進行合併或
agreements that restrict the payment of dividends by subsidiaries or         整合；及(ix)削減分擔抵押品中的抵押權益。契約亦
the repayment of inter-company loans and advances, (viii) engage in          包含有關違約事件的若干慣常條文。
mergers or consolidations and (ix) impair the security interests in
the Shared Collateral. The Indenture also contains certain customary

Credit Risk                                                                   信貸風險
Credit risk is the risk of financial loss to the Group if a customer or       信貸風險為倘金融工具的客戶或交易對手未能履行
counterparty to a financial instrument fails to meet its contractual          其合約責任而令本集團承受財務虧損的風險，主要
obligations and arises principally from the Group’s receivables from          來自本集團應收客戶的款項。最高的風險水平限於
customers. Maximum exposure is limited to the carrying amounts of             本集團的綜合中期財務報表所呈列金融資產的賬面
the financial assets presented in the Group’s consolidated interim            值。
financial statements.

The Group’s exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by the               本集團的信貸風險水平主要受到每名客戶個別的特
individual characteristics of each customer. However, management              點影響。然而，管理層亦會考慮其客戶群的結構，
also considers the demographics of its customer base, including               包括客戶從事業務經營所屬行業及所在國家的違約
the default risk of the industry and the country in which customers           風險，因為此等因素可能對信貸風險構成影響。
operate, as these factors may have an influence on credit risk.

The Group has established a credit policy under which each new                本集團已制定信貸政策，據此，本集團會在向每名
customer is analyzed individually for credit worthiness before the            新客戶提供標準的付款和交付條款與條件前，個別
Group’s standard payment and delivery terms and conditions are                地對其信譽進行分析。
offered.

In monitoring customer credit risk, customers are grouped according           本集團在監察客戶的信貸風險時，乃根據客戶的信
to their credit characteristics, including aging profile and the existence    貸特點（包括賬齡概況，以及之前是否存有財政困
of previous financial difficulties. Trade and other receivables relate        難）將客戶分組。應收賬款及其他應收款項主要與
mainly to the Group’s wholesale customers. Customers that are                 本集團的批發客戶有關。被評級為「高風險」的客戶
graded as “high risk” are placed on credit hold and monitored by the          的信貸會被暫擱及由本集團進行監察，未來的銷售
Group and future sales are made on an approval basis.                         需要經過審批方可進行。

subsidiaries. No other guarantees have been made to third parties.            無向第三方作出其他擔保。

operating activities, invested cash, available lines of credit and, subject   量、投資現金、可用信貸額及本公司增發股份（惟
to shareholder approval, the Company’s ability to issue additional            須待股東批准後方可作實）的能力。本公司相信，
shares. The Company believes that its existing cash and estimated             其現有現金及估計現金流量，加上流動營運資金，

adversely affected by foreign currency exchange rate volatility relative    司一般採用當地貨幣為其功能貨幣。本集團定期使

1.    Background                                                         1.      背景
      Samsonite International S.A. (the “Company”), together with its            新 秀 麗 國 際 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」， 連 同
      consolidated subsidiaries (the “Group”), is principally engaged            其綜合附屬公司統稱為「本集團」）主要
      in the design, manufacture, sourcing and distribution of                   在全球從事設計、製造、採購及分銷行
      luggage, business and computer bags, outdoor and casual bags               李箱、商務包及電腦包、戶外包及休
      and travel accessories throughout the world, primarily under               閒包以及旅遊配件，旗下經營的品牌
      the Samsonite ®, Tumi ®, American Tourister ®, Gregory ®, High             主要包括新秀麗 ® 、Tumi ® 、American
      Sierra ®, Lipault ® and Hartmann ® brand names as well as other            Tourister ®、Gregory ®、High Sierra ®、
      owned and licensed brand names. The Group sells its products               Lipault ® 及Hartmann ® 品牌以及其他自有
      through a variety of wholesale distribution channels, through              及獲授權的品牌。本集團透過各種批發
      its company-operated retail stores and through e-commerce.                 分銷渠道、自營零售店及透過電子商貿
      The Group sells its products in Asia, North America, Europe and            銷售其產品。本集團於亞洲、北美洲、
      Latin America.                                                             歐洲及拉丁美洲銷售其產品。

      The Company’s ordinary shares are listed on the Main Board                 本公司的普通股於香港聯合交易所有限
      of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                     公 司（「 聯 交 所 」）主 板 上 市 。 本 公 司 於
      Exchange”). The Company was incorporated in Luxembourg on                  2011 年3 月8 日於盧森堡註冊成立為一
      March 8, 2011, as a public limited liability company (a société            家公眾有限責任公司，其註冊辦事處位
      anonyme), whose registered office is 13-15 avenue de la Liberté,           於13-15 avenue de la Liberté, L-1931
      L-1931 Luxembourg.                                                         Luxembourg。

      This condensed consolidated interim financial information                  本簡明綜合中期財務資料於 2024 年 8 月
      was authorized for issuance by the Company’s Board of                      14 日經本公司董事會（「董事會」）授權刊
      Directors (the “Board”) on August 14, 2024, and is unaudited.              發，但未經審計。本公司的核數師KPMG

      accordance with auditing standards generally accepted in                   利堅合眾國公認核數準則以及按照《國際
      the United States of America applicable to reviews of interim              審閱委聘準則》第2410號由實體獨立核數

(a)   Statement of Compliance                                            (a)     合規聲明
      The condensed consolidated interim financial statements have               簡明綜合中期財務報表是根據《國際會計
      been prepared in accordance with International Accounting                  準則》第34 號中期財務報告 及《香港聯合
      Standard 34, Interim Financial Reporting , and the applicable              交易所有限公司證券上市規則》的適用披
      disclosure provisions of the Rules Governing the Listing of                露條文編製（「綜合中期財務報表」或「綜
      Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the                 合中期財務資料」）。綜合中期財務報表
      “consolidated interim financial statements” or “consolidated               應與本集團於2023年12月31日及截至該
      interim financial information”). The consolidated interim                  日止年度的經審計綜合財務報表一併閱
      financial statements should be read in conjunction with the                讀，該等報表是根據國際會計準則理事
      Group’s audited consolidated financial statements as of and for            會（「IASB」）頒佈的《國際財務報告準則》
      the year ended December 31, 2023, which have been prepared                （「IFRS」）會計準則編製。該總稱包括所
      in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards             有《國際會計準則》  （「IAS」）及相關詮釋。
      (“IFRS”) Accounting Standards, as issued by the International

      reporting period. As part of its amendments, the IASB has                     IASB已撤除無條件權利的要求，現時要

      covenants) do not affect a liability’s classification at that date.           而，當非流動負債受未來契約約束時，

      leverage ratio of the Company and its restricted subsidiaries             的第一留置權淨槓桿比率或本公司的企

      Term Loan A Facility and the Revolving Credit Facility was                通及循環信貸融通的適用息差為1.125%
      1.125% (or a base rate plus 0.125%).                                      （或基準利率另加0.125%）。

      1.75% per annum). The interest rate payable on the 2024 Term              （或基準利率另加年利率 1.75%）。2024

      As the Company’s Term Loan A Facility, Revolving Credit                   由於本公司的A定期貸款融通、循環信貸
      Facility and 2024 Term Loan B Facility have floating interest             融通及 2024 年 B 定期貸款融通具有浮動

      effect for the relevant period.

      In addition to paying interest on the outstanding principal               除支付優先信貸融通項下的未償還借款

      be a future outflow of funds and, once established, whether a          相關的撥備是否足夠。本集團基於其過往經

      and circumstances known at each reporting date. The provision          般及行政開支中確認。當結算承擔的日期不

18. Income Taxes                                                                18. 所得稅
(a)   Taxation in the Condensed Consolidated Statements                         (a)      於簡明綜合收益表中的稅項
      of Income
      For interim reporting purposes, the Group applied the effective                    就中期呈報而言，本集團計算中期期間除所
      tax rate to profit before income tax for the interim period. The                   得稅前溢利時應用實際稅率。所呈報的實際
      reported effective tax rate was calculated using a weighted                        稅率按本集團應繳納稅項的司法權區之加權
      average income tax rate from those jurisdictions in which                          平均所得稅率計算，並就永久性賬面 ╱ 稅
      the Group is subject to tax, adjusted for permanent book/                          務差異、稅項優惠、稅務儲備變動及尚未確

      changes in unrecognized deferred tax assets. The effective tax                     際稅率是基於管理層對預期整個財政年度的

      income for the period adjusted for certain discrete items for the
      period.

21. Financial Instruments                                                 21. 金融工具
(a)   Fair Value Versus Carrying Amounts                                  (a)     公允價值與賬面值的比較
      All financial assets and liabilities have fair values that                  所有金融資產及負債的公允價值與賬面值相
      approximate carrying amounts.                                               若。


(b)   Fair Value of Financial Instruments                                 (b)     金融工具之公允價值
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                   公允價值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序交
      asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction             易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付的價
      between market participants at the measurement date.                        格。IFRS會計準則建立一套公允價值等級
      IFRS Accounting Standards establish a fair value hierarchy                  架構，該架構排列用以計量公允價值的估值
      that prioritizes the inputs to valuation techniques used to                 方法輸入數據的優先等級。該等級架構給予
      measure fair value. The hierarchy gives the highest priority to             於活躍市場中相同資產或負債的未經調整報
      unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for identical assets             價最高等級（第一級別計量），以及涉及重
      or liabilities (Level 1 measurements) and the lowest priority to            大不可觀察輸入數據的計量最低等級（第三
      measurements involving significant unobservable inputs (Level               級別計量）。公允價值等級架構的三個級別
      3 measurements). The three levels of the fair value hierarchy               如下：
      are as follows:

      •      Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active              •       第一級別輸入數據為本集團有能力
             markets for identical assets or liabilities that the Group                   於計量日取得的相同資產或負債於
             has the ability to access at the measurement date.                           活躍市場的報價（未經調整）。


      •      Level 2 inputs are inputs other than quoted prices                   •       第二級別輸入數據為不包括第一級
             included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset                    別的報價的資產或負債的可直接或
             or liability, either directly or indirectly.                                 間接觀察的輸入數據。


      •      Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or              •       第三級別輸入數據為資產或負債的
             liability.                                                                   不可觀察輸入數據。


      The level in the fair value hierarchy within which a fair                   公允價值計量整體在公允價值等級架構中的
      measurement in its entirety falls is based on the lowest level              層級分類，乃基於對公允價值計量整體而言
      input that is significant to the fair value measurement in its              屬重大的最低層級輸入數據。
      entirety.

      There were no transfers between the levels of the fair value                截至2024年6月30日止六個月，用於計量金
      hierarchy used in measuring the fair value of financial                     融工具之公允價值的公允價值等級架構級別
      instruments and there were no changes in the classification of              之間並無轉變，而金融資產分類亦無變化。
      financial assets during the six months ended June 30, 2024.

      The carrying amount of cash and cash equivalents, trade                     由於現金及現金等價物、應收賬款、應付賬
      receivables, accounts payable, short-term debt, and accrued                 項、短期債務及應計開支的到期日或年期較
      expenses approximates fair value because of the short maturity              短，因此，該等工具的賬面值與公允價值相
      or duration of these instruments.                                           若。

The Board of Directors (the “Board”) of the Company is pleased to           本公司董事會（「董事會」）欣然呈列截至2024年6月
present this Corporate Governance Report for the six months ended           30日止六個月的本企業管治報告。
